Even though you get the sense they don't like each other particularly, this is a meeting ground of sorts. Kenzo: I've not met him.

%% u4804:0-20 %%
Delwath: I... Why do you ask?

%% u4805:0-49, u4806:0-9, u4807:0-14, u4808:0-7, u4809:0-37, u4810:0-23, u4811:0-19 %%
DM: I heard what you were saying about corruption and the tree. Made me wonder because the gods have been quiet for a while. Made me think maybe you might have met Deni.

%% u4812:0-5, u4813:0-11 %%
Delwath: Deni? Who's that?

%% u4814:0-11, u4815:0-65, u4816:0-33, u4817:0-13 %%
DM: They are... I guess the best translation would be the spirit of the redwoods. The word is Tanshi in our tongue. A god of kind.

%% u4818:0-11 %%
Delwath: A small one.

%% u4819:0-4, u4820:0-12, u4821:0-32 %%
DM: Yes, a little god is the word I would use, I guess.

%% u4822:0-28 %%
Delwath: You said they've been quiet?

%% u4823:0-3 %%
DM: Yes.

%% u4824:0-78 %%
Wellby: That's not the first time we've heard the story of gods becoming quiet, is it?

%% u4825:0-33, u4826:0-5 %%
Delwath: It is not. It was Shakun who went quiet.

%% u4827:0-70 %%
Wellby: But also the gods of the peaceful orcs stopped talking to them, right?

%% u4828:0-14, u4829:0-23 %%
DM: Who is Shakun? Is that another Tanshi?

%% u4830:0-5 %%
Wellby: Sorry.

%% u4831:0-27 %%
Delwath: The god of the warlike orcs.

%% u4832:0-16 %%
Wellby: Oh, right, right.

%% u4833:0-26 %%
Seeker: Shakun is a god of Dunmar.

%% u4834:0-19 %%
Kenzo: So, very quiet one.

%% u4835:0-18 %%
Delwath: A mean god. Sorry.

%% u4836:0-13 %%
Seeker: We helped her.

%% u4837:0-63 %%
Wellby: You're put out of the village because you don't follow the elf?

%% u4838:0-22 %%
DM: That's insightful, one.

%% u4839:0-59 %%
Seeker: Because, what did you say? Because you don't follow the elf.

%% u4840:0-26, u4841:0-13, u4842:0-13 %%
Wellby: We don't follow him either. That's a joke. That's a joke.

%% u4843:0-33 %%
Seeker: But why don't you follow the elf?

%% u4844:0-12 %%
Wellby: But we don't.

%% u4845:0-29, u4846:0-23 %%
Seeker: Why don't you follow the elf? We'd like to learn more.

%% u4847:0-2 %%
Wellby: Mm.

%% u4848:0-18 %%
DM: I trust the Tanshi.

%% u4849:0-3, u4850:0-33 %%
Delwath: The Tanshi under the sway of the elf?

%% u4851:0-23, u4852:0-42 %%
DM: The Tanshi won't speak. Did you see all the offerings on the tree?

%% u4853:0-19, u4854:0-39 %%
Seeker: I did inspect them. Those offerings, they're for the Tanshi.

%% u4855:0-36, u4856:0-18 %%
DM: It's been years since one was taken. And they are like-

%% u4857:0-35, u4858:0-40 %%

%% u4859:0-4 %%
DM: Nope.

%% u4860:0-8, u4861:0-32, u4862:0-40 %%
Delwath: I, um... Are you familiar, are you, uh... Have you studied the lore of the Tanshi?

%% u4863:0-7, u4864:0-12 %%
DM: Indeed. Quite deeply.

%% u4865:0-34, u4866:0-19 %%
Delwath: You've heard of one called Yezali? From many years ago.

%% u4867:0-9 %%
DM: She looks.

%% u4868:0-46 %%
Delwath: They would be known to be lost to you, I think.

%% u4869:0-16, u4870:0-44, u4871:0-23, u4872:0-5, u4873:0-33, u4874:0-24, u4875:0-38, u4876:0-55, u4877:0-3 %%
DM: She looks, um... She kind of looks around a little bit, like, looks against the door. Um... I do not know that specific name. I fear many may be lost. She's, like, looking around nervously. I do not know this is the place to speak of such things. Hmm.

%% u4878:0-21 %%
Delwath: Where might we speak?

%% u4879:0-11 %%
Wellby: At the tree.

%% u4880:0-25 %%
DM: Well, if Deni is there...

%% u4881:0-35, u4882:0-5, u4883:0-31 %%

%% u4884:0-5 %%

%% u4885:0-9 %%
DM: I'm Theba.

%% u4886:0-6 %%
Delwath: Theba?

%% u4887:0-5, u4888:0-13, u4889:0-31, u4890:0-56 %%

%% u4891:0-9 %%
Delwath: Hmm, yes.

%% u4892:0-21 %%
Kenzo: Do you know the tree?

%% u4893:0-12, u4894:0-23, u4895:0-49 %%

%% u4896:0-3 %%
Delwath: Hmm.

%% u4897:0-18, u4898:0-26, u4899:0-41, u4900:0-11, u4901:0-43, u4902:0-79, u4903:0-13, u4904:0-16 %%
DM: Through the woods. I have not heard anything. I have not left the village in some time. I would not necessarily want to venture into the woods. She kind of gestures to her lack of armor and weapon. Things are not always safe in the depths of the Tinitaka.

%% u4905:0-20 %%
Seeker: What does that mean?

%% u4906:0-13 %%
DM: Mm. I forget.

%% u4907:0-13, u4908:0-28 %%

%% u4909:0-13 %%
Seeker: The Tinitaka.

%% u4910:0-37, u4911:0-9, u4912:0-10, u4913:0-16, u4914:0-76 %%

%% u4915:0-7 %%
Seeker: Mm-hmm.

%% u4916:0-73 %%
DM: Or the Elderwood for those with no patience for the full names of things.

%% u4917:0-51 %%
Seeker: The names of things can be instructive, can't they?

%% u4918:0-15 %%
Kenzo: Or distracting.

%% u4919:0-35 %%
Wellby: You know where the other trees are?

%% u4920:0-10, u4921:0-47 %%

%% u4922:0-15 %%
Seeker: How old is she?

%% u4923:0-20 %%
Kenzo: Just gonna ask that.

%% u4924:0-15 %%
DM: Like, late 30s.

%% u4925:0-3 %%
Seeker: Oh.

%% u4926:0-36 %%

%% u4927:0-3 %%
Seeker: Um,

%% u4928:0-59 %%

%% u4929:0-46 %%

%% u4930:0-6 %%
Seeker: We are.

%% u4931:0-18, u4932:0-45 %%

%% u4933:0-30 %%

%% u4934:0-10, u4935:0-63 %%

%% u4936:0-39 %%

%% u4937:0-3 %%

%% u4938:0-17 %%
DM: Mezzar asks much.

%% u4939:0-42 %%

%% u4942:0-3 %%
Wellby: Hm.

%% u4943:0-30 %%
DM: I have not had the experience.

%% u4944:0-38 %%

%% u4945:0-44, u4946:0-20 %%

%% u4947:0-26 %%
Wellby: Are there others like you?

%% u4948:0-11 %%

%% u4949:0-3 %%
Wellby: Hm.

%% u4952:0-49 %%

%% u4953:0-4 %%

%% u4954:0-16 %%
DM: Who is not clear.

%% u4955:0-24, u4956:0-15 %%

%% u4957:0-30 %%

%% u4958:0-8 %%

%% u4959:0-9 %%

%% u4961:0-7 %%

%% u4962:0-58 %%

%% u4963:0-10, u4964:0-20, u4965:0-5 %%

%% u4966:0-19 %%

%% u4968:0-12 %%

%% u5025:0-47, u5026:0-37, u5027:0-80, u5028:0-41 %%

%% u5029:0-7, u5030:0-38, u5031:0-35 %%

%% u5032:0-5, u5033:0-18 %%

%% u5034:0-5 %%

%% u5039:0-4 %%

%% u5040:0-26 %%

%% u5041:0-31 %%

DM: Itar was chosen by Mezzar.

But, when I was in communion with the tree, when I looked up into the eaves of the tree and the branches, and it felt like it opened up to me, and I saw elven children in the tree playing, 30, 40 of them. Many.

%% u5112:0-4 %%
Kenzo: Hmm.

Delwath: He just kind of retreats to the... I did not expect to ever see elven children again.

%% u5141:0-5, u5142:0-18 %%

%% u5144:0-8 %%
